| `\AoEndless` | Over the whole 27.56\,h stream (5,567 utterances, one process, no restart, no reset) the resident memory of H22ctl was 62.35\,MB at the start, 69.3\,MB once loaded, at most 69.53\,MB, and 67.85\,MB at the end; its word lag over 95,710 matched words was 310\,ms median and 439\,ms at the 95th percentile. It scored 9.89\% pooled WER against 8.94\% with each utterance decoded alone by the same engine, 9.85\% in its first half and 9.95\% in its second: a constant cost of continuous decoding, not a drift. In the first hour (204 utterances) the cost was +0.95\,pp (bootstrap bounds +0.37 to +1.56), +0.62\,pp of it from the pauses' audio in the encoder's left context. Resetting the state after 1.5\,s without output and replaying the last 1.0\,s brings that hour to 8.51\% (+0.05\,pp, $-$0.43 to +0.54; 9.36\% without the reset in the same build), with 0 false words in its pauses. The 27.56\,h run with the reset on was still going when this draft was built. | sentence | exploratory | endless, results sentence | eval-lane's endless stream (27.56 h, 5,567 DEV utterances once, seeded pauses) (46db60b97976…) | docs/paper/data/raw/alwayson/endless/endless27h.t0.run.json; docs/paper/data/raw/alwayson/endless/endless27h.t0.scored.summary.json; docs/paper/data/raw/alwayson/endless/endless27h.t0.rss.jsonl; docs/paper/data/raw/alwayson/stream/hour1/isolated-v-stream.json; docs/paper/data/raw/alwayson/stream/hour1/p.isolated-v-diagA.json; docs/paper/data/raw/alwayson/stream/replay/p.isolated-v-T0.json; docs/paper/data/raw/alwayson/stream/replay/p.isolated-v-T1500R1000.json; docs/paper/data/raw/alwayson/stream/replay/hour1-replay-t1500_r1000.summary.json; docs/paper/data/raw/export/H22ctl-79800-ema.fulldev.pytorch-v-engine.json |  |
